## 2026-10-08 objective reset: closure-first trading loop

The current objective is deliberately narrowed from broad institutional feature
parity to one usable A-share closure path. The near-term acceptance target is:

`fixed strategy -> target portfolio -> paper order lifecycle -> explicit account/order/fill evidence -> same Engine/Ledger reconciliation`

The institutional capability matrix remains a reference map, not a reason to
expand infrastructure or delay the closure path. Existing paper lifecycle,
manual export preview/import/reconciliation, T+1/cash/position controls,
prefill formatting and readback templates are reused; they must not be rebuilt
under new names.

### Current priorities

1. **P0, implementable without live quotes:** keep the unified release-readiness
   view, paper order lifecycle, recovery states, explicit THS export contract,
   idempotent observation import and same-ledger reconciliation consumable from
   the workbench.
2. **P0, external evidence dependency:** obtain a verified THS account page,
   same-session raw RMB quotes, cash and frozen cash, sellable/T+1 state,
   structured orders and structured trades. Missing or ambiguous fields remain
   blocked; do not infer zero orders or zero fills.
3. **P1 research:** continue fixed-snapshot strategy comparison and data-source
   recovery, but do not promote a strategy or retune from the latest visible
   result while account/execution evidence is incomplete.
4. **Deferred P2:** advanced child-order algorithms, low-latency institutional
   infrastructure, broad factor search and additional UI polish stay behind
   the closure path unless a concrete consumer requires them.

## 20261014 P1 shortline limit-up continuation baseline

The first bounded shortline research artifact is
`outputs/shortline_research/20261014_limitup_continuation/`. It uses the
extended StockDB raw daily snapshot through `2026-10-13`, the project price
limit rules and non-ST rows to build descriptive stage labels. Historical
stage-1 events reached four/five boards at `9.21%/6.93%`; stage-2 events at
`33.63%/25.30%`; stage-3 events at `66.21%/49.80%`.

These are conditional label rates, not strategy returns or executable fill
rates. The first baseline deliberately lacks listing-date filtering, sector
mapping, minute/order-book data and queue/fill evidence. The next experiment
must compare naive stage baselines against market-regime, sector-relative and
daily liquidity features with time-ordered validation, T+1 and limit-up
non-fill constraints.

The corrected shortline model now also has a bounded shared Engine/Ledger
candidate-panel rehearsal at
`outputs/shortline_research/20261014_limitup_continuation/candidate_panel_replay_2026ytd_top5_20symbols/`.
It selects close-available model scores only, takes Top5 per day, applies a
complete raw-bar path gate, and caps the panel at 20 symbols. The 183-session
2026-YTD panel retained 39 events, generated 29 orders and 56 fills, and ended
with `+13.2055%` net return and `-12.0953%` maximum drawdown under the shared
fees, lot, T+1 and limit-up rules. This is a candidate-panel rehearsal, not
full-market or untouched validation; `future_labels_used_for_score=false`,
`ledger_mutated=false`, `strategy_validated=false`, and
`execution_admitted=false`. The replay is indexed by the read-only diagnostics
catalog as `shortline_candidate_panel_replay_diagnostic`, with selection still
disabled. The next comparison is a fresh purged period and the next-open
touchability proxy; no paper or THS handoff is admitted from this result.

The same bounded configuration was split without changing Top5/day, the
20-symbol cap, holdings, costs or execution rules. The 2026 H1 receipt
returned `+1.8194%` with `-8.1109%` MDD; the 2026 H2 receipt returned
`-2.3036%` with `-4.3649%` MDD. This weakens stability of the full-window
positive result and is retained as a time-split diagnostic, not a retuned
strategy comparison.

The fixed temporal-split consumer is now saved at
`outputs/shortline_research/20261014_limitup_continuation/temporal_split_early_later_2026/`.
It joins the existing daily OHLC touchability proxy only after replay: all 33

## 20261012 P0 same-Ledger consumer consistency repair

All key paper/operator, risk, release-control, observation-bundle, session
handoff, and simulated-ledger readiness views now derive their flat
`same_ledger_reconciliation_status` and `same_ledger_imported` fields from the
detailed account/order/fill/cashflow reconciliation view. The legacy journal
receipt remains exposed separately as `ths_journal_reconciliation`; it can no
longer override a detailed result such as `isolated_rehearsal_only`,
`reconciliation_required`, or `ledger_reconciled`. This prevents the workbench
from displaying a prior journal import as if the current four-table observation
had already reconciled. No broker operation or Ledger mutation was performed.
The session-handoff consumer now carries the same detailed object, so recovery
and next-session review use the identical order-level evidence.
Each broker order row also exposes read-only effective fill price, fill rate,
fee bps and, when the saved plan has an explicit limit price, direction-aware
adverse slippage; missing benchmarks remain null rather than inferred.

## 20261012 P0/P1 account-risk release gate

`trading/ths/account_risk.py` now accepts the same detailed reconciliation
receipt used by the workbench.  Account arithmetic remains visible when the
cash/position export is valid, but `account_ready` and the read-only order-risk
preview stay blocked for `structured_package_not_ready`,
`isolated_rehearsal_only` or `reconciliation_required`.  Only
`ledger_reconciled` or explicit empty-activity evidence clears this gate.  The
API passes this receipt to both account-risk consumers, so a prior or isolated
journal result cannot release a new order preview.  No broker I/O, order
submission or Ledger mutation was performed.

The execution-evidence and selected-plan consumers also expose
`ths_observed_fill_tca_v1`: aggregate filled quantity, gross, fees, fill rate,
effective fill price and reference-price slippage are derived only from the
same bundle rows.  Missing fills or missing reference prices remain
`not_available`/`null`; rehearsal rows remain `isolated_rehearsal_only`.

The read-only observation-health consumer now also emits structured
`alert_records` with severity, evidence source and next action alongside its
backward-compatible alert codes.  This is an operator handoff/monitoring
surface only; it does not poll THS, submit orders or mutate the Ledger.

## 20261012 P0/P1 selected-plan release-gates consumer

`GET /api/trading/operator-package/selected-plan` now exposes one
`offline_selected_plan_release_gates_v1` object joining strategy promotion,
research admission, account risk, order risk, same-Ledger reconciliation and
paper release. It includes the first blocking gate and its concrete next
action, while preserving the detailed evidence below each gate. This removes
the need for the workbench to infer release state by joining separate views;
the result remains paper/read-only and `execution_admitted` is always false.

The strategy-promotion builder now consumes saved `family-holdout` receipts by
exact candidate signal. Waiting, failed and completed holdout states are
carried into the matching review row and the global evidence list; a holdout
for another strategy cannot satisfy the selected candidate. The current
`stock_mature_month_family_return` receipt is still waiting for a source newer
than `2026-09-30`, so no candidate is promoted.

The evidence API and selected-plan research view now carry the same global
holdout evidence list, so the operator UI can see a waiting or completed
holdout without reading the artifact directly. This remains informational;
`eligible_candidate_count` and `execution_admitted` stay unchanged until the
matching candidate, account evidence and explicit review decision all exist.

The offline opening runner also now preserves `opening_pack.selected_strategy_id`
when a separate paper-selection receipt is absent. This fixes a real P0 state
handoff failure where the desk snapshot was valid but the package builder
rejected it as a strategy mismatch. The refreshed default package for

## 20261012 P0 same-Ledger broker bundle join

The workbench now exposes `GET /api/trading/operator-package/ledger-reconciliation`
and embeds the same read-only result in the operator package, paper release
readiness, lifecycle, and account-risk views. It joins a complete THS
account/orders/trades/cumulative-cashflow bundle to the same `ths_plans` receipt
by broker contract ID and reports per-order symbol, side, quantity, gross,
fees, signed cash delta, import receipt, and exact mismatch codes. It never
imports, refreshes, submits, cancels, or mutates the Ledger.

The join also carries the four-table linkage defects into the same result:
orphan trades, orphan cashflows, missing cumulative cashflows, fill-quantity
conflicts, identity conflicts, and status/fill conflicts are no longer hidden
behind a missing Ledger plan.

The result distinguishes `ledger_reconciled`, `reconciliation_required`,
`explicit_empty_activity_confirmed`, and `isolated_rehearsal_only`. The current
local evidence is still `isolated_rehearsal_only` with no native account
export, no live order/fill/cashflow evidence, and no Ledger mutation; this is
an external evidence gap, not a passed account closure. Paper release readiness
now turns `reconciliation_required`, an incomplete bundle, or isolated rehearsal
into an explicit blocker instead of letting the workbench imply that a paper
preview is account-ready. The account-order-risk response now carries the full
same-Ledger detail, so pre-trade risk and reconciliation cannot silently diverge.
Focused API and account-risk tests pass (`38 passed`).

The same detailed reconciliation object is now also carried by the execution-
evidence, portfolio-controls, release-controls, observation-journal-status and
simulated-Ledger-readiness consumers. These views remain read-only and retain
the same `execution_admitted=false` boundary; no page can fall back to only a
historical journal flag while hiding per-order mismatch details. Focused API
and account-risk tests pass (`41 passed`).

The observation-health view now also returns the underlying per-order issue
codes alongside its alert list, so stale/incomplete broker evidence can be
acted on without another endpoint lookup; it still performs no broker I/O and
does not mutate the Ledger.

It now embeds the complete `ths_same_ledger_bundle_reconciliation_v1` detail
under the health result as well. The P2 operator monitor can therefore carry
the same order-level linkage, cashflow, fill and admission evidence as the P0
risk/release consumers instead of exposing only a summary. Focused API and
account-risk tests pass (`43 passed`).

The complete-package read model now preserves its observation window and
order-level linkage when exposing a validated four-table export. This closes a
real P0 false-empty path: after an idempotent bundle import, the same-Ledger
consumer now matches the persisted plan by trading date and broker contract
instead of dropping to `explicit_empty_activity_confirmed`. The focused
import/reconciliation path passes with `matched_count=1`.

The following-session reconciliation endpoint now returns that same detailed
bundle result after applying the session receipt, so recovery cannot finish
with a paper balance update while hiding an order-level broker mismatch.

P2 observation health is now callable from the existing CLI as
`run_committee.py ths observation-health --out <health.json>`. The command
reuses the API read-only view and writes no account, journal or Ledger state;
the current receipt remains `attention_required` because the saved native
observation is stale and the structured orders/trades/cashflows are absent.

The remaining order-desk consumers (`basket-schedule`, `blotter` and
`execution-attribution`) now carry the same detailed reconciliation object as
the risk/release pages. A current offline-package readback returned all six
consumer views successfully with `same_ledger_detail=true` and
`execution_admitted=false`.

without suppressing a later revision that shares the same timestamp. This
keeps CLI and recovery writes consistent with the four-table batch import while
preserving existing cumulative-observation semantics.

The workbench operator package now also exposes a compact read-only
`ths_journal_reconciliation_view_v1`. It joins each saved THS plan's broker
observation timestamps, account cash/position reconciliation flags, and the
explicit same-Ledger import receipt beside the structured four-table package.
An empty journal is reported as `no_saved_plans`; it never becomes a zero-order

`same_ledger_reconciliation_status`, `same_ledger_imported` and

receipt; current release readiness remains blocked when the native four-table
package is absent.

The replay catalog and candidate board now retain the LightGBM test-split
diagnostics and paired deltas for each saved seed: feature count, best
iteration, Rank-IC mean/std, HAC t-statistic, and the net-return, drawdown and
turnover difference versus the fixed rule. These fields are research evidence
only and remain explicitly `research_only=true` / `execution_admitted=false`.
The candidate board also distinguishes `research_comparison_eligible` for
non-overlapping StockDB/Tencent paired samples and multi-window stability
artifacts; this is a comparison-only flag and does not enable selection or
execution.
Pinned replay integrity failures are no longer silently dropped from the
catalog when an artifact root or planned ledger set changes; the operator
caller receives the integrity error so a damaged comparison cannot look like a
valid but smaller research set.

Completion is now reported by evidence level: paper-consumer complete,
read-only broker evidence complete, manual prefill/readback complete, and
submitted/fill/reconciled complete. A paper artifact or test count is never
reported as live readiness. Routine hash audits, repeated smoke checks and
unchanged status scans are not queue work.

The lifecycle, session-handoff, decision-queue, account-risk, execution-evidence,
portfolio-controls, observation-journal and simulated-ledger-readiness consumers now
reuse the same read-only `ths_journal_reconciliation_view_v1` receipt as the
top-level operator package and paper-preview readiness. A prior explicit
Ledger import is therefore visible consistently as
`same_ledger_reconciliation_status`, `same_ledger_imported` and

broker/client actions. The current local runtime has no saved THS plans, so

`/api/trading/quote-observation/readiness`, `/initialize` and `/import` now
consume the fixed `D:/QuantificationData/trading/ths/quote_observation` drop
directory. Initialization creates only empty templates for the symbols in the

timestamp and complete symbol coverage; an incomplete or mismatched-date quote
set cannot clear the release blocker. The workbench exposes the same state and
does not prefill, submit, or mutate the Ledger.

unsupported existing positions remain visible as `carry_forward_scope_excluded`
but cannot create sell orders or quote requirements in a mainboard-only handoff.

the frozen paper order set changes. A template containing any price is never
overwritten; this prevents a stale blank template from silently omitting new
paper orders while preserving manual work.

`operator_action_board.csv` beside the institutional function matrix. It is

orders, trades and cumulative cashflows without initializing the THS journal or

After that preview is ready, `ths import-observation-bundle --confirm-import`
can atomically save the four validated user-export observations into the
existing `ths_observations` journal. Exact account/view/timestamp/date repeats

observation evidence only and leaves plans, `runs.state`, fills and execution
admission unchanged.
For a saved manual-review plan, `ths reconcile-observation-bundle --id ...
--confirm-import` now performs the same idempotent observation import and then
calls the existing plan refresh, exposing the existing filled/partial/conflict
state without any client operation or unified-ledger write.

## Oct7 Alpha158 loss attribution points to turnover before new factors

The saved-account attribution separated the two completed Alpha158 cases. The
fixed Ridge account lost RMB 42,577.35 and remained deeply negative after the
fixed-fill fee/open-price addback, so its primary problem is signal/selection.
The all-seed rank mean lost RMB 2,168.15; its fixed-fill addback was RMB
6,469.11, with 547 fills, RMB 5,532.55 of matched raw-open price difference,
and 39 minimum-commission fills. This is not a counterfactual zero-cost return,
but it prioritizes turnover reduction and rank-hysteresis experiments before
adding more Alpha158 features. Artifact:
`outputs/loss_attribution/20261007_093309_7e2faf8d/REPORT.md`.

The same all-seed account also went through the existing execution-gap
observer. It found 330 target/attempt records, 16 nonzero residual gaps, 12
legal-order-quantity flags, 2 prior-volume-participation flags and 2 suspended
attempts. Target-removal exits were 48.2% of filled turnover and new entries
50.6%; mean total target gap was 8.07% of equity, with 4.47% attributable to
execution quantity gaps. This supports a fixed rank-buffer/turnover-reduction
experiment as the next research run, not another factor expansion. Artifact:
`outputs/turnover_attribution/20261007_093426_4e94bec9/REPORT.md`.

The first fixed buffer experiment is now complete: `retain_rank 8 -> 12`
reduced turnover from RMB 5,460,401.77 to RMB 5,015,224.07 and fills from 547
to 514, but net return fell from `-1.55%` to `-3.63%` and MDD worsened to
`-32.15%`. It is retained as a negative result and is not an opening
candidate; the next effort should test portfolio-level turnover/cost handling
or use the stronger fixed rule, not widen the rank buffer again. Artifact:
`outputs/market_replay/20261007_093806_7561b3b8/REPORT.md`.

The 12-case same-window comparison confirmed `stock_equal_three_families` as
the only descriptive nondominated case: on 2026-01-05..2026-09-30 it returned
`+3.26%`, MDD `-8.77%`, Sharpe `0.3854`, with zero stale marks, while the
Alpha158 all-seed rank mean returned `-1.55%` and MDD `-31.32%`. The fixed-rule
10/9 paper pack now carries these metrics beside its 32 candidate lines; it
still has no saved orders/fills and remains paper-only. Comparison:
`outputs/research_comparisons/20261007_094015_c1d9b89f/REPORT.md`; opening
pack: `outputs/opening_pack/20261007_014048_08b11a32/REPORT.md`.

## Oct7 Alpha158 fixed execution-delay diagnostic completes

The fixed Alpha158 family was evaluated on the pinned 2024--2026 feature
snapshot with 158 definitions, 666 signal dates and the predeclared 1/5/10/20/60
day horizons. Extra delay shifted both entry and exit by 0/1/2 exchange
sessions; no model fit, account replay, selection search or trade admission was
performed. In the inspected 2026 test segment, the median IC change across all
158 definitions was `-0.004038` after one extra session and `-0.004911` after
two extra sessions for the 1-day horizon. The median quintile-excess change
was `-0.002535` and `-0.005712` respectively. Longer horizons were less
uniform: the 5-day and 20-day IC medians also declined, while some test
quintile excesses improved; this is diagnostic evidence, not a causal account
P&L estimate. The result supports reducing execution delay and avoiding
short-horizon turnover, but does not promote Alpha158 or explain the full
negative factor replay.

Artifact: `outputs/execution_delay/20261007_074452_a3b81913/REPORT.md`;
data: `D:/QuantificationData/trading/research/execution_delay/20261007_074452_a3b81913`.

ablation and inherits the source replay's rights/merger settlement policies.
On the Templates leave-out account, the fixed minimum-commission internal
reweight band changed net return by only `+0.0009` percentage points, fees by
`-RMB5.29` and turnover by `-RMB1,068`, while preserving `-9.17%` MDD and
`0.9547` Sharpe. The result rules out small internal reweights as the main
cause of the candidate's observed advantage; it is still an inspected,
research-only account path with no holdout or broker admission.

Artifact: `outputs/factor_candidate_cost_replays/20261007_080636_b615035a/REPORT.md`.

including its declared `floor` ordinary-share allocation assumption. It

The actionable research focus shifts to factor information quality, tail

## Oct6 current-score provenance guards

`trading/ths/signals.py::validate_current_score_bundle` now rejects a current EOD
receipt whose local observation date predates the declared decision session, and
`build_current_request` rejects a score batch generated before its source receipt
was observed. These checks prevent old or causally reversed timestamps from being
relabeled as a current score batch; they do not create a current source or grant
execution admission. Targeted signal verification passed (`52 passed`) and
`py_compile` passed.

## Oct6 family holdout immutability guard

`family_holdout.check` now refuses to inspect or reset a completed holdout, so
an old candidate snapshot cannot erase a completed fresh-holdout receipt. When
a not-yet-complete holdout falls back to the candidate cutoff, stale checked
inputs and validation fields are removed. Targeted family-holdout verification
passed (`4 passed`) and `py_compile` passed; this is a state-integrity guard,
not new holdout or execution evidence.

## Oct6 THS cross-session read guard

`THSAdapter.read` now checks the local market date again after the inspected
view has been read. A capture that crosses midnight is rejected instead of
persisting a prior-session page under the new observation timestamp. The
focused account/order/trade read tests plus rollover regression passed
(`4 passed, 69 deselected`); this is a local read-integrity safeguard, not a
physical client observation or live execution result.

## Oct6 THS cashflow observation boundary correction

`trading/ths/journal.py::refresh` now applies the same next-plan/session
observation cutoff to `cashflows` that it already applied to `account`
snapshots. This prevents a later cumulative cashflow export from being used
as evidence for an earlier plan. The focused boundary regressions passed
(`2 passed, 70 deselected`); this is a local ledger-protection change only,
with no physical THS observation or live execution admission.

## 20261012 P0 structured-evidence source manifest

The THS structured-observation boundary now records a source manifest for
each of the four required exports (`account`, `orders`, `trades`, and
`cashflows`), including presence, byte size, and SHA-256. The API verifies
the manifest read-only before same-Ledger comparison and changes the
reconciliation state to `reconciliation_required` if an export is modified
after capture. Missing exports remain unknown; they are never converted to
zero activity. The persisted package itself now also carries
`source_manifest_status=complete`, so downstream consumers do not need to
reconstruct that field from the embedded manifest.

The isolated rehearsal package was rebuilt at
`D:\QuantificationData\trading\research\ths_bundle_rehearsal\20261012_source_manifest_v2\structured_observation_package_v1.json`.
It is complete for the four-table rehearsal, reports
`source_manifest_status=verified` at the API boundary, and remains
`isolated_rehearsal_only`, `ledger_mutated=false`, and
`execution_admitted=false`. Native THS evidence is still partial and is not
silently promoted into that rehearsal.

The selected-plan API also normalizes legacy semicolon-delimited promotion
blockers into arrays; this prevents persisted review text from becoming
one-character blocker entries. Focused validation after this change:
`67 passed` across the THS bundle export, operator package, and account-risk
tests, with blocker normalization, changed-source reconciliation, and
account-risk source-integrity assertions included. Account-risk consumers now
also block directly on `source_manifest_status=source_changed`, so a changed
four-table export cannot be used for sizing even before the detailed join is
reviewed.

The observation-bundle readiness and selected-plan consumers now expose the
verified source-manifest status and issue list as first-class fields. The
workbench can therefore distinguish blank templates, a verified complete
package, and a changed source bundle without opening the package file or
silently treating the isolated rehearsal as a user-account export.

The readiness endpoint now computes that field from the current standard
four-file directory only. Blank or invalid templates report `not_ready` rather
than inheriting `verified` from the newest isolated rehearsal package.
The readiness regression was exercised with the existing operator-package
suite (`27 passed`), including the blank-template boundary.

The institutional capability scan now verifies the package source manifest and
requires `source_scope=persistent_user_export` before classifying the P0
four-table capability as complete. The current scan therefore correctly
reports `partial_paper_only` for the `native_readonly_receipt` package instead
of treating an isolated/native read-only package as an account closure.

## 20261012 P0 account-risk source boundary tightening

The account-risk consumer now requires `source_manifest_status=verified` for a
persistent four-table user export. A missing or unverified manifest is a hard
`structured_source_manifest_unverified` blocker; a changed manifest retains the
more specific `structured_source_manifest_changed` blocker. This prevents a
copied or incomplete export from becoming account-ready merely because its
numeric fields happen to parse.

The low-level order-risk calculation still supports the immutable pre-submit
account snapshot used by the existing paper Ledger path, but it now labels that
input `pre_submit_account_snapshot` / `pre_submit_snapshot_only` and keeps it
separate from persistent broker evidence. Focused account-risk and Ledger
import validation: `59 passed`.

The same-Ledger bundle join now applies the identical manifest rule: a
`persistent_user_export` with no verified source manifest returns
`source_manifest_unverified` and cannot be compared or treated as reconciled.
The isolated rehearsal path remains available for paper diagnostics. Focused
API/account-risk validation after this repair: `53 passed`.

`D:\Quantification\outputs\capability_gap_scans\20261011_220707_2101fbf6\run.json`.
